# DAY 34: Dining Out, Restaurant Etiquette & Food Preferences
### बाहर भोजन करना, रेस्तरां शिष्टाचार एवं खान-पान की प्राथमिकताएँ
- **CEFR Level:** A2.1 | **Estimated Duration:** 40 mins
- **Prerequisites:** Days 31–33 (Politeness, Modals & Request Structures)

### 1. Learning Objectives
**English:**
1. Navigate all stages of dining out in English: making reservations, ordering courses, asking for recommendations, and settling the bill.
2. Articulate dietary restrictions, food allergies, and preferences (vegetarian, vegan, gluten-free, spicy level) with absolute precision.
3. Formulate polite requests to waitstaff using 'Could we have...', 'I'll have...', and 'Would it be possible to...'.
4. Address service errors or food issues diplomatically without aggressive confrontation.
5. Eliminate direct translation traps common in Indian restaurant interactions ('Give bill', 'Parcel this').

**Hindi (उद्देश्य):**
1. रेस्तरां के सभी चरणों को अंग्रेजी में संभालना: टेबल बुक करना, खाना ऑर्डर करना, सुझाव मांगना और बिल चुकाना।
2. खान-पान की पाबंदियों, एलर्जी और प्राथमिकताओं (शाकाहारी, वीगन, ग्लूटेन-फ्री, मिर्च का स्तर) को सटीकता से बताना।
3. 'Could we have...', 'I'll have...' का उपयोग करके वेटर से शिष्टता से बात करना।
4. खाने या बिल में किसी गलती को बिना किसी असभ्यता के कूटनीतिक और शांत तरीके से ठीक कराना।
5. भारतीय रेस्तरां संदर्भों के अनुवाद की गलतियों (जैसे 'Give bill', 'Parcel this') को वैश्विक रूप में बदलना।

### 2. Pedagogical Instruction (Teach)
#### Hindi Explanation:
दिन 34 में आपका स्वागत है! रेस्तरां में खाना ऑर्डर करते समय और वेटर से बात करते समय शिष्टाचार अत्यंत महत्वपूर्ण होता है। भारत में अक्सर सीधे आदेश जैसे 'Bring water', 'Give menu', 'Make it fast' बोल दिए जाते हैं, जो वैश्विक अंग्रेजी में बेहद रूखे माने जाते हैं।

1. टेबल बुक करना (Reservations):
   - 'I'd like to book a table for four for tonight at 8:00 PM.'

2. ऑर्डर देना (Ordering Food):
   - कभी भी 'Give me burger' न कहें।
   - कहें: 'I'll have the grilled paneer, please.' या 'Could I get the vegetable pasta?'

3. खान-पान की प्राथमिकताएँ और एलर्जी (Dietary Preferences & Allergies):
   - 'I am vegetarian / vegan.' (मैं शाकाहारी / वीगन हूँ)।
   - 'Is this dish gluten-free?' (क्या यह ग्लूटेन-मुक्त है?)
   - 'Could you please make it mild / medium spicy?' (क्या आप इसमें मिर्च कम रख सकते हैं?)
   - 'I am allergic to peanuts.' (मुझे मूंगफली से एलर्जी है)।

4. बचा हुआ खाना पैक कराना (Takeaway / Leftovers):
   - 'Parcel this' केवल भारतीय अंग्रेजी है।
   - वैश्विक अंग्रेजी: 'Could we get this packed to go, please?' या 'Could I have a doggy bag / takeout box?'

5. बिल माँगना (Requesting the Bill):
   - 'Give bill' ➔ 'Could we have the bill / check, please?'
   - 'Can we split the bill?' (क्या हम अलग-अलग आधा-आधा भुगतान कर सकते हैं?)।

#### English Explanation:
Welcome to Day 34! Dining out in English is a structured social ritual characterized by diplomatic request formulas, precise dietary vocabulary, and polite transaction management.

Key Stages of the Restaurant Journey:
1. Reservation and Arrival:
   - 'A table for [Number], under the name of [Name], please.'
   - 'Do you have an outdoor table available?'
2. Ordering Protocols:
   - Use future-intention 'I'll have...' or polite modal requests 'Could I get...?' (Avoid raw imperatives like '*Bring me...*').
   - Starter / Appetizer ➔ Main Course / Entrée ➔ Dessert ➔ Beverages.
3. Dietary Specifications & Health Constraints:
   - 'Does this dish contain dairy/nuts?'
   - 'I am lactose-intolerant / allergic to shellfish.'
4. Rectifying Issues Diplomatically:
   - 'Excuse me, I believe we ordered the salad without onions.' (Polite softening with 'I believe').
5. Settling the Account:
   - 'Could we have the check/bill, please?'
   - 'Do you take card/contactless payments?'
   - 'We would like to split the bill evenly.'

#### Core Grammar & Pragmatic Rules:
- **Rule 1: The 'I'll have' Ordering Syntax** [Culinary Pragmatics]
  Use 'I'll have + [Dish/Drink]' for spontaneous dining decisions at the point of ordering (NOT '*I want to eat...*' or '*Give me...*').
- **Rule 2: Allergy and Intolerance Predicates** [Medical/Dietary Accuracy]
  Structure: 'I am allergic to + [Food item]' (Adjective + Preposition 'to'). 'I have a nut allergy' (Noun).
- **Rule 3: Diplomatic Complaint Framing** [Service Diplomacy]
  Preface food issues with polite markers: 'Excuse me, I'm afraid this soup is a bit cold' rather than direct confrontation.
- **Rule 4: Global Lexicon for Leftovers** [Global Fluency]
  Replace colloquial Indianisms ('parcel this') with standard international expressions: 'Could you box this up for us?' or 'Can I get this to go?'.

#### Formula Matrix:
| Subject Group | Formula Structure | English Example | Hindi Meaning |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **Ordering Food** | `I'll have / Could I please get + [Dish] + with [Side/Modification]?` | I'll have the Margherita pizza with extra basil, please. | मैं अतिरिक्त तुलसी के साथ मार्गेरिटा पिज़्ज़ा लूँगा। |
| **Dietary Clarification** | `Does this contain [Ingredient]? / Is this [Dietary Tag]?` | Does this curry contain any dairy or cream? | क्या इस करी में कोई डेयरी उत्पाद या मलाई शामिल है? |
| **Requesting Recommendation** | `What do you recommend for [Course/Preference]?` | What do you recommend for the main course? | मुख्य भोजन (मेन कोर्स) के लिए आप क्या सुझाव देते हैं? |
| **Requesting Bill** | `Could we have the bill / check, please?` | Could we have the bill, please? We're paying by card. | क्या हमें बिल मिल सकता है? हम कार्ड से भुगतान कर रहे हैं। |

#### L1 Hindi Speaker Transfer Trap:
- **Trap Name:** The 'Give Menu', 'Parcel It', & 'Give Bill' Habits
- **Common Hindi-Interference Pattern:**
```
1. Saying '*Give water*' or '*Bring bill*'.
2. Saying '*Please parcel this food*'.
3. Saying '*Is non-veg available?*' instead of asking for meat/poultry options.
```
- **Why This Fails in English:** Western service staff expect conversational modal requests ('Could we have the bill?'). 'Parcel' in English means a postal package. Use 'to go' or 'boxed up'.
- **Natural Authentic English Remediation:** 1. 'Could we please have some water?' | 2. 'Could we get this packed to go?' | 3. 'Could we have the bill, please?'

### 3. Core Lexicon & Conversational Formulas (Learn)
#### High-Yield Vocabulary:
| Word | Part of Speech | IPA | Hindi Meaning | Example Sentence |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **Appetizer** | *Noun* | `/ˈæp.ə.taɪ.zər/` | शुरुआती नाश्ता / स्टार्टर (भोजन से पहले) | We ordered garlic bread as an appetizer. *(हमने स्टार्टर के रूप में गार्लिक ब्रेड का ऑर्डर दिया।)* |
| **Entrée** | *Noun* | `/ˈɒn.treɪ/` | मुख्य भोजन (मेन कोर्स) | For my entrée, I chose the grilled salmon. *(अपने मुख्य भोजन के लिए मैंने ग्रिल्ड सैल्मन चुना।)* |
| **Beverage** | *Noun* | `/ˈbev.ər.ɪdʒ/` | पेय पदार्थ (ड्रिंक) | Hot and cold beverages are listed on page three. *(गर्म और ठंडे पेय तीसरे पृष्ठ पर सूचीबद्ध हैं।)* |
| **Allergic** | *Adjective* | `/əˈlɜː.dʒɪk/` | एलर्जी से ग्रस्त | I am severely allergic to peanuts and tree nuts. *(मुझे मूंगफली और मेवों से गंभीर एलर्जी है।)* |
| **Gluten-free** | *Adjective* | `/ˌɡluː.tənˈfriː/` | ग्लूटेन-मुक्त (गेहूँ रहित) | Do you have any gluten-free pasta options? *(क्या आपके पास कोई ग्लूटेन-मुक्त पास्ता विकल्प है?)* |
| **Vegetarian** | *Noun/Adj* | `/ˌvedʒ.ɪˈteə.ri.ən/` | शाकाहारी | Are all these dishes strictly vegetarian? *(क्या ये सभी व्यंजन पूरी तरह से शाकाहारी हैं?)* |
| **Vegan** | *Noun/Adj* | `/ˈviː.ɡən/` | शुद्ध वीगन (पशु व डेयरी उत्पादों से रहित) | She follows a strict vegan diet without any milk or butter. *(वह दूध या मक्खन के बिना एक सख्त वीगन आहार का पालन करती है।)* |
| **Reservation** | *Noun* | `/ˌrez.əˈveɪ.ʃən/` | टेबल की पूर्व बुकिंग | I would like to make a dinner reservation for four people. *(मैं चार लोगों के लिए डिनर का आरक्षण कराना चाहूँगा।)* |
| **Recommendation** | *Noun* | `/ˌrek.ə.menˈdeɪ.ʃən/` | सिफारिश / सुझाव | What is the chef's special recommendation today? *(आज शेफ़ का विशेष सुझाव क्या है?)* |
| **Splitting** | *Noun* | `/ˈsplɪt.ɪŋ/` | बिल को आपस में बाँटना | We would prefer splitting the bill evenly between us. *(हम आपस में बिल को बराबर बांटना पसंद करेंगे।)* |

#### Essential Pragmatic Phrases:
- **"Could we have a table for two, please?"** [Arrival Formula]
  *अर्थ:* क्या हमें दो लोगों के लिए एक टेबल मिल सकती है?
- **"I'll have the soup of the day, please."** [Ordering]
  *अर्थ:* मैं आज का सूप लूँगा।
- **"Could you make it less spicy, please?"** [Food Modification]
  *अर्थ:* क्या आप इसे कम तीखा बना सकते हैं?
- **"Could we get this packed to go?"** [Leftovers Request]
  *अर्थ:* क्या आप इसे पैक कर सकते हैं?
- **"Could we have the check, please?"** [Payment Request]
  *अर्थ:* क्या हमें बिल मिल सकता है?
- **"Keep the change, thank you!"** [Tipping]
  *अर्थ:* बाकी खुले पैसे आप रख लीजिए, धन्यवाद!

### 4. Bilingual Exemplar Models
1. **EN:** Good evening, we have a reservation under the name of Sharma for 7:30.
   **HI:** शुभ संध्या, हमारे नाम (शर्मा) पर शाम 7:30 बजे का आरक्षण है।
2. **EN:** Could we please see the dessert menu before we decide?
   **HI:** क्या निर्णय लेने से पहले हम मिठाई का मेनू देख सकते हैं?
3. **EN:** I'll start with the tomato soup and have the vegetable risotto as my main course.
   **HI:** मैं टमाटर के सूप से शुरुआत करूँगा और मेन कोर्स में वेजिटेबल रिसोट्टो लूँगा।
4. **EN:** Does this dressing contain eggs or mayonnaise?
   **HI:** क्या इस सलाद ड्रेसिंग में अंडे या मेयोनेज़ हैं?
5. **EN:** Could you please serve the sauce on the side?
   **HI:** क्या आप कृपया सॉस को अलग से परोस सकते हैं?
6. **EN:** Excuse me, I think there is a slight mistake on our bill.
   **HI:** माफ़ कीजिएगा, मुझे लगता है कि हमारे बिल में एक छोटी सी गलती है।
7. **EN:** We would like to split the bill between three credit cards.
   **HI:** हम तीन क्रेडिट कार्डों के बीच बिल को विभाजित करना चाहते हैं।
8. **EN:** Everything was delicious; our compliments to the chef!
   **HI:** सब कुछ बहुत स्वादिष्ट था; शेफ़ को हमारी ओर से बधाई!
9. **EN:** Can we have a bottle of sparkling water for the table, please?
   **HI:** क्या टेबल के लिए हमें स्पार्कलिंग पानी की एक बोतल मिल सकती है?
10. **EN:** We haven't finished eating yet; could you give us five more minutes?
   **HI:** हमने अभी खाना पूरा नहीं किया है; क्या आप हमें पाँच मिनट और दे सकते हैं?
11. **EN:** Is it possible to substitute fries with a side salad?
   **HI:** क्या फ़्राइज़ की जगह साइड सलाद लेना संभव है?
12. **EN:** Could you box up the remaining pizza slices to go, please?
   **HI:** क्या आप बचे हुए पिज़्ज़ा के स्लाइस पैक कर सकते हैं?

### 5. Pronunciation & Phonological Calibration
- **Target Sounds & Suprasegmentals:** Pronunciation of French culinary loanwords: 'Entrée' /ˈɒn.treɪ/, 'Risotto' /rɪˈzɒt.əʊ/, 'Buffet' /ˈbʊf.eɪ/; Smooth reduction of 'Could we have' /kʊdwiːˈhæv/
- **Phonetic Trap:** Do not pronounce the 't' at the end of 'buffet' or 'valet'. Do not say 'non-veg' in international Western restaurants; use 'meat', 'poultry', or 'seafood'.
**Audio Drills:**
- `[day34_audio_001]` (TEACHER): "Could we have the bill, please?"
- `[day34_audio_002]` (TEACHER): "I'll have the vegetarian entrée with a side salad."
- `[day34_audio_003]` (TEACHER): "Does this dessert contain any peanuts?"
- `[day34_audio_004]` (TEACHER): "Could we get this packed to go?"

### 6. Interactive Practice Drills (35 Items)
**Exercise 1** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: ORDERING_FOOD)
- **Prompt:** Choose the most polite and natural way to order a dish at a restaurant:
- **Options:** `I'll have the vegetable biryani, please.` | `Give me vegetable biryani.` | `I want to eat vegetable biryani.` | `Bring vegetable biryani fast.`
- **Correct Answer:** `I'll have the vegetable biryani, please.`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'I'll have [Dish], please' is the standard polite English ordering formula.
- **Explanation (HI):** रेस्तरां में ऑर्डर देने के लिए 'I'll have [Dish], please' सबसे सही और शिष्ट तरीका है।

**Exercise 2** [ERROR_CORRECTION] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: PRAGMATICS)
- **Prompt:** Correct the Indian English restaurant request into global English: 'Please parcel this leftover food.'
- **Correct Answer:** `Could you please pack this to go?`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Pack this to go' or 'box this up' is global standard English; 'parcel' is an Indianism.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'Parcel this' की जगह 'Could you please pack this to go?' या 'box this up' कहें।

**Exercise 3** [FILL_IN_BLANK] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: DIETARY_TERMS)
- **Prompt:** Complete the allergy statement: 'I am severely ______ (allergy / allergic) to peanuts.'
- **Correct Answer:** `allergic`
- **Explanation (EN):** The predicate adjective is 'allergic' ('allergic to').
- **Explanation (HI):** विशेषण के रूप में 'allergic to' आता है।

**Exercise 4** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: PAYMENT_REQUESTS)
- **Prompt:** How do you politely ask for the check at the end of the meal?
- **Options:** `Could we have the bill, please?` | `Give bill please.` | `Bring the check right now.` | `How much money we must pay?`
- **Correct Answer:** `Could we have the bill, please?`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Could we have the bill/check, please?' is the standard polite request.
- **Explanation (HI):** बिल माँगने का सबसे शिष्ट तरीका: 'Could we have the bill, please?'

**Exercise 5** [WORD_REORDER] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: RESERVATIONS)
- **Prompt:** Order: [reservation / have / a / we / under / the name of Roy]
- **Options:** `We have a reservation under the name of Roy.` | `A reservation we have under the name of Roy.` | `Under the name of Roy have we a reservation.` | `We under the name of Roy have a reservation.`
- **Correct Answer:** `We have a reservation under the name of Roy.`
- **Explanation (EN):** We + have a reservation + under the name of Roy.
- **Explanation (HI):** सही क्रम: We have a reservation under the name of Roy.

**Exercise 6** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: VOCABULARY)
- **Prompt:** What is a dish eaten at the start of a meal called?
- **Options:** `Appetizer / Starter` | `Dessert` | `Beverage` | `Main Course`
- **Correct Answer:** `Appetizer / Starter`
- **Explanation (EN):** The opening course is an appetizer or starter.
- **Explanation (HI):** शुरुआती भोजन को 'Appetizer' या 'Starter' कहते हैं।

**Exercise 7** [ERROR_CORRECTION] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: POLITENESS)
- **Prompt:** Fix the blunt request: 'Bring me water.'
- **Correct Answer:** `Could I have a glass of water, please?`
- **Explanation (EN):** Replace the bare imperative with 'Could I have a glass of water, please?'.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'Could I have a glass of water, please?' कहें।

**Exercise 8** [FILL_IN_BLANK] (Difficulty: medium | Tag: RESTAURANT_VOCAB)
- **Prompt:** Fill in the blank with (substitute / change): 'Is it possible to ______ the fries with a salad?'
- **Correct Answer:** `substitute`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Substitute A with B' means replace A with B.
- **Explanation (HI):** बदलने के लिए 'substitute' का प्रयोग होता है।

**Exercise 9** [HINDI_TO_ENGLISH] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: TRANSLATION_HI_EN)
- **Prompt:** Translate: 'क्या आप इसमें मिर्च कम रख सकते हैं?'
- **Options:** `Could you make it mild, please?` | `Make chili less in this.` | `Don't put mirchi in food.` | `Is this less spicy?`
- **Correct Answer:** `Could you make it mild, please?`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Could you make it mild, please?' or 'less spicy' is natural and polite.
- **Explanation (HI):** सही अनुवाद: 'Could you make it mild, please?'

**Exercise 10** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: PAYMENT_VOCAB)
- **Prompt:** What does 'splitting the bill' mean?
- **Options:** `Dividing the total cost among the diners.` | `Tearing the paper bill.` | `Refusing to pay the bill.` | `Paying only for drinks.`
- **Correct Answer:** `Dividing the total cost among the diners.`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Splitting the bill' means sharing the expense equally.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'Splitting the bill' का अर्थ है बिल का खर्च आपस में बांटना।

**Exercise 11** [ERROR_CORRECTION] (Difficulty: medium | Tag: SERVICE_DIPLOMACY)
- **Prompt:** Fix the diplomatic complaint: 'You brought wrong food!'
- **Correct Answer:** `Excuse me, I believe I ordered the vegetarian pasta.`
- **Explanation (EN):** Use softening markers ('Excuse me, I believe...') for diplomatic feedback.
- **Explanation (HI):** कूटनीतिक रूप से कहें: 'Excuse me, I believe I ordered...'

**Exercise 12** [FILL_IN_BLANK] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: DIETARY_TERMS)
- **Prompt:** Complete the sentence: 'Does this dessert contain any ______ (dairy / dairies) products?'
- **Correct Answer:** `dairy`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Dairy' functions as an adjective modifying 'products'.
- **Explanation (HI):** दूध उत्पादों के लिए 'dairy products' आता है।

**Exercise 13** [WORD_REORDER] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: SENTENCE_BUILDING)
- **Prompt:** Order: [the chef / compliments / our / to / please give]
- **Options:** `Please give our compliments to the chef.` | `Our compliments please give to the chef.` | `To the chef please give our compliments.` | `Give please our compliments to the chef.`
- **Correct Answer:** `Please give our compliments to the chef.`
- **Explanation (EN):** Please give + our compliments + to the chef.
- **Explanation (HI):** सही क्रम: Please give our compliments to the chef.

**Exercise 14** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: RECOMMENDATIONS)
- **Prompt:** Choose the correct phrase to ask for the server's personal recommendation:
- **Options:** `What do you recommend as a main course?` | `Which food you will give me?` | `What you like to eat here?` | `Tell me the best thing now.`
- **Correct Answer:** `What do you recommend as a main course?`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'What do you recommend...?' is standard restaurant etiquette.
- **Explanation (HI):** सुझाव पूछने के लिए 'What do you recommend...?' सही है।

**Exercise 15** [ENGLISH_TO_HINDI] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: TRANSLATION_EN_HI)
- **Prompt:** What is the meaning of: 'Keep the change'?
- **Options:** `बाकी खुले पैसे आप रख लीजिए।` | `मुझे खुले पैसे वापस दीजिए।` | `पैसे बदल लीजिए।` | `बिल दोबारा बनाइए।`
- **Correct Answer:** `बाकी खुले पैसे आप रख लीजिए।`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Keep the change' tells the server that leftover change is their tip.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'Keep the change' का अर्थ है 'बाकी खुले पैसे आप (टिप के रूप में) रख लीजिए'।

**Exercise 16** [FILL_IN_BLANK] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: RESERVATIONS)
- **Prompt:** Complete the table request: 'Could we have a table ______ (for / of) four near the window?'
- **Correct Answer:** `for`
- **Explanation (EN):** The collocation is 'a table for [Number]'.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'A table for four' (चार लोगों के लिए टेबल) सही है।

**Exercise 17** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: DIETARY_TERMS)
- **Prompt:** Which dietary choice avoids ALL animal products including honey, dairy, and eggs?
- **Options:** `Vegan` | `Vegetarian` | `Pescatarian` | `Gluten-free`
- **Correct Answer:** `Vegan`
- **Explanation (EN):** A vegan diet excludes all animal-derived foods and ingredients.
- **Explanation (HI):** वीगन (Vegan) आहार में किसी भी प्रकार के पशु उत्पाद (दूध, मक्खन, शहद आदि) शामिल नहीं होते।

**Exercise 18** [ERROR_CORRECTION] (Difficulty: medium | Tag: GLOBAL_ENGLISH)
- **Prompt:** Fix the phrasing: 'Do you have non-veg food?' (In a London restaurant)
- **Correct Answer:** `What meat or poultry options do you have on the menu?`
- **Explanation (EN):** In Western restaurants, ask for 'meat, poultry, or seafood options' instead of 'non-veg'.
- **Explanation (HI):** अंतर्राष्ट्रीय संदर्भ में 'non-veg' के स्थान पर 'meat/poultry/seafood' का प्रयोग करें।

**Exercise 19** [DIALOGUE_COMPLETION] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: ORDERING_FOOD)
- **Prompt:** Complete the dialogue: Server: 'Are you ready to order, sir?' Customer: 'Yes, ______ have the grilled vegetable platter, please.'
- **Correct Answer:** `I'll`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'I'll have...' is the standard formula.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'I'll have...' आएगा।

**Exercise 20** [WORD_REORDER] (Difficulty: medium | Tag: FOOD_MODIFICATION)
- **Prompt:** Order: [the side / could / have / the dressing / we / on / ?]
- **Options:** `Could we have the dressing on the side?` | `Could have we the dressing on the side?` | `On the side could we have the dressing?` | `The dressing on the side could we have?`
- **Correct Answer:** `Could we have the dressing on the side?`
- **Explanation (EN):** Could we have + the dressing + on the side?
- **Explanation (HI):** सही क्रम: Could we have the dressing on the side?

**Exercise 21** [FILL_IN_BLANK] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: PREPOSITIONS)
- **Prompt:** Fill in the blank with the correct preposition: 'I am allergic ______ (to / with) shellfish.'
- **Correct Answer:** `to`
- **Explanation (EN):** The correct preposition is 'allergic TO'.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'Allergic' के साथ 'to' आता है ('allergic to')।

**Exercise 22** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: SPELLING)
- **Prompt:** What is the sweet dish eaten at the very end of a meal called?
- **Options:** `Dessert` | `Desert` | `Starter` | `Appetizer`
- **Correct Answer:** `Dessert`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Dessert' (/dɪˈzɜːt/) with double 's' is the sweet course.
- **Explanation (HI):** खाने के बाद की मिठाई को 'Dessert' कहते हैं।

**Exercise 23** [ERROR_CORRECTION] (Difficulty: medium | Tag: PAYMENT_VOCAB)
- **Prompt:** Correct the sentence: 'Can we pay by cash or we must use card?'
- **Correct Answer:** `Can we pay in cash or do we need to use a card?`
- **Explanation (EN):** Use 'pay in cash' or 'pay by card'.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'Pay in cash' या 'pay by card' सही रूप है।

**Exercise 24** [HINDI_TO_ENGLISH] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: TRANSLATION_HI_EN)
- **Prompt:** Translate: 'क्या आप बचा हुआ खाना पैक कर सकते हैं?'
- **Options:** `Could you box up the leftovers, please?` | `Parcel the remaining food.` | `Make parcel of this.` | `Wrap food for home.`
- **Correct Answer:** `Could you box up the leftovers, please?`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Could you box up the leftovers, please?' is natural and polite.
- **Explanation (HI):** सही अनुवाद: 'Could you box up the leftovers, please?'

**Exercise 25** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: PHONETICS)
- **Prompt:** What is the silent letter in 'Buffet' (/ˈbʊfeɪ/)?
- **Options:** `t` | `u` | `f` | `e`
- **Correct Answer:** `t`
- **Explanation (EN):** The final 't' in 'buffet' is silent.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'Buffet' में अंतिम 't' मूक (silent) होता है।

**Exercise 26** [FILL_IN_BLANK] (Difficulty: medium | Tag: RESTAURANT_VOCAB)
- **Prompt:** Complete the request: 'Could we get a ______ (jug / pitcher) of tap water for the table, please?'
- **Correct Answer:** `pitcher`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Pitcher' or 'carafe' / 'bottle' is common dining vocabulary.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'Pitcher' या 'bottle' सही शब्द है।

**Exercise 27** [WORD_REORDER] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: RECOMMENDATIONS)
- **Prompt:** Order: [recommend / what / you / for / dessert / do / ?]
- **Options:** `What do you recommend for dessert?` | `What you recommend for dessert do?` | `For dessert what do you recommend?` | `Do what you recommend for dessert?`
- **Correct Answer:** `What do you recommend for dessert?`
- **Explanation (EN):** What + do + you + recommend + for dessert?
- **Explanation (HI):** सही क्रम: What do you recommend for dessert?

**Exercise 28** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: SERVICE_DIPLOMACY)
- **Prompt:** Choose the best phrase to check on a delayed food order politely:
- **Options:** `Excuse me, could you please check on our main courses?` | `Where is our food? It is too late!` | `Why the chef is sleeping?` | `Bring food right now.`
- **Correct Answer:** `Excuse me, could you please check on our main courses?`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Excuse me, could you please check on...?' is professional and courteous.
- **Explanation (HI):** सभ्य पूछताछ: 'Excuse me, could you please check on our main courses?'

**Exercise 29** [ERROR_CORRECTION] (Difficulty: medium | Tag: STATIVE_VERBS)
- **Prompt:** Fix the error: 'I am fasting, so I am wanting only fruits.'
- **Correct Answer:** `I am fasting, so I would like only fresh fruits, please.`
- **Explanation (EN):** Use 'I would like' instead of dynamic '*I am wanting*'.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'I am wanting' की जगह 'I would like...' का प्रयोग करें।

**Exercise 30** [FILL_IN_BLANK] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: IDIOMS)
- **Prompt:** Complete the sentence: 'Everything was exquisite; please extend our compliments to the ______ (chef / cook).' (High restaurant praise)
- **Correct Answer:** `chef`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Compliments to the chef' is the traditional culinary idiom.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'Compliments to the chef' सही मुहावरा है।

**Exercise 31** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: BEVERAGE_TERMS)
- **Prompt:** Which term refers to water served with natural fizz/carbonation?
- **Options:** `Sparkling water` | `Still water` | `Tap water` | `Boiled water`
- **Correct Answer:** `Sparkling water`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Sparkling water' is carbonated/fizzy water.
- **Explanation (HI):** सोडा युक्त बुलबुले वाले पानी को 'Sparkling water' कहते हैं।

**Exercise 32** [HINDI_TO_ENGLISH] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: TRANSLATION_HI_EN)
- **Prompt:** Translate: 'हम बिल का आधा-आधा भुगतान करना चाहेंगे।'
- **Options:** `We would like to split the bill evenly.` | `We want half half bill.` | `Cut bill in two pieces.` | `We divide payment 50-50.`
- **Correct Answer:** `We would like to split the bill evenly.`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'We would like to split the bill evenly' is the standard idiomatic expression.
- **Explanation (HI):** सही अनुवाद: 'We would like to split the bill evenly.'

**Exercise 33** [SPOKEN_RESPONSE] (Difficulty: medium | Tag: SPEAKING)
- **Prompt:** Speak aloud: You are at a restaurant. Order: 1. A starter (tomato soup), 2. A main course (paneer tikka with naan), 3. Mention that you have a severe peanut allergy, 4. Ask for still water.
- **Correct Answer:** `A fluent 4-step restaurant ordering sequence.`
- **Explanation (EN):** Integrates starter, main, allergy notification, and beverage requests smoothly.
- **Explanation (HI):** स्टार्टर, मेन कोर्स, एलर्जी की सूचना और पानी का ऑर्डर शिष्टता से दें।

**Exercise 34** [RECALL] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: RECALL)
- **Prompt:** What is the global English replacement for the Indian slang 'parcel this food'?
- **Correct Answer:** `pack this to go`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Pack this to go' or 'box this up' is the standard global expression.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'Parcel this' की जगह 'pack this to go' या 'box this up' कहा जाता है।

**Exercise 35** [RECALL] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: RECALL)
- **Prompt:** What is the correct preposition in 'allergic ___ nuts'?
- **Correct Answer:** `to`
- **Explanation (EN):** The collocation is strictly 'allergic to'.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'Allergic' के साथ हमेशा 'to' आता है।

### 7. Authentic Communicative Application (Use)
- **Real-World Context:** A customer, Neha, dines at an upscale Italian restaurant in Mumbai and interacts with the server, Marco, regarding gluten-free options and settling the bill.
**Mastery Dialogue:**
- **Marco:** "Good evening! Welcome to Bella Italia. Are you ready to order, or would you like a few more minutes with the menu?"
  *(हिंदी: "शुभ संध्या! बेला इटालिया में आपका स्वागत है। क्या आप ऑर्डर देने के लिए तैयार हैं, या आपको मेनू देखने के लिए कुछ और मिनट चाहिए?")*
- **Neha:** "We're ready, thank you. Could you tell me if your risotto is gluten-free?"
  *(हिंदी: "हम तैयार हैं, धन्यवाद। क्या आप बता सकते हैं कि आपका रिसोट्टो ग्लूटेन-मुक्त है?")*
- **Marco:** "Yes, absolutely! All our risottos are prepared with arborio rice and are 100% gluten-free."
  *(हिंदी: "हाँ, बिल्कुल! हमारे सभी रिसोट्टो आर्बोरियो चावल से बनाए जाते हैं और 100% ग्लूटेन-मुक्त हैं।")*
- **Neha:** "Wonderful! I'll have the mushroom risotto, and my friend will have the penne arrabbiata. Could we also have some sparkling water?"
  *(हिंदी: "बहुत बढ़िया! मैं मशरूम रिसोट्टो लूँगी, और मेरी सहेली पेने अराबीआता लेगी। क्या हमें थोड़ा स्पार्कलिंग पानी भी मिल सकता है?")*
- **Marco:** "Certainly. I'll place that order right away."
  *(हिंदी: "बिल्कुल। मैं आपका ऑर्डर अभी लगा देता हूँ।")*

**Spoken Production Challenge:**
- **Task (EN):** Simulate a 1-minute roleplay calling a French restaurant to reserve a table for six for your parents' anniversary, asking about vegetarian choices.
- **Task (HI):** अपने माता-पिता की शादी की सालगिरह पर छह लोगों की टेबल बुक करने और शाकाहारी विकल्पों के बारे में पूछने का अभ्यास करें।

### 8. Rigorous Assessment & Verification
**Assessment Query 1** [Skill: Pragmatic Competence]
- **Question:** Why is 'I'll have...' grammatically and pragmatically preferred over 'I want...' or bare imperatives when ordering in restaurants?
- **Model Analytical Benchmark:** 'I'll have...' utilizes spontaneous future modal decision-making that conveys polite intention without demanding or treating the waitstaff like subordinates, honoring international service etiquette.

**Assessment Query 2** [Skill: Dietary Literacy]
- **Question:** Explain the difference between 'Vegetarian' and 'Vegan' in international culinary terminology.
- **Model Analytical Benchmark:** 'Vegetarian' diets exclude meat, poultry, and fish, but usually include dairy (milk, cheese, butter) and sometimes eggs. 'Vegan' diets strictly exclude all animal-derived foods and by-products (meat, seafood, dairy, eggs, honey, gelatin).

**Assessment Query 3** [Skill: Dispute Diplomacy]
- **Question:** Construct a polite three-part formula for pointing out an overcharge on a restaurant bill.
- **Model Analytical Benchmark:** 1. Polite attention-getter ('Excuse me'), 2. Softened assertion of possible discrepancy ('I think there might be a slight confusion on our bill'), 3. Specific clarification ('We were charged for two desserts, but we only ordered one').

### 9. Spaced Repetition Protocol (Review)
- **SRS Module Tag:** `STAGE_4_DINING_ETIQUETTE`
- **Spaced Retrieval Cadence:** Days 1, 3, 7, 14, 30

# DAY 35: Time Management, Appointments & Travel Scheduling
### समय प्रबंधन, अपॉइंटमेंट एवं यात्रा शेड्यूलिंग
- **CEFR Level:** A2.1 | **Estimated Duration:** 40 mins
- **Prerequisites:** Days 31–34 (Social & Functional Communication)

### 1. Learning Objectives
**English:**
1. Schedule, reschedule, confirm, and cancel professional appointments and medical consultations with diplomatic precision.
2. Master precise temporal prepositions: 'At' (exact times), 'On' (days/dates), 'In' (months/years/durations), 'By' (deadlines), and 'Until' (continuation).
3. Navigate airport, railway, and metro transit systems (boarding, delays, connections, baggage claims).
4. Express time availability, scheduling conflicts, and punctuality updates without ambiguity.
5. Eliminate direct mother-tongue translation errors in calendar and deadline expressions.

**Hindi (उद्देश्य):**
1. डॉक्टर या व्यावसायिक मुलाकातों (Appointments) का समय तय करना, बदलना (Reschedule), पुष्टि करना और रद्द करना।
2. समय से जुड़े सटीक Prepositions: 'At' (सटीक समय), 'On' (दिन/तारीख), 'In' (महीना/साल/अवधि), 'By' (अंतिम समय सीमा), और 'Until' (तक)।
3. हवाई अड्डे, रेलवे स्टेशन और मेट्रो यात्रा (बोर्डिंग, देरी, कनेक्टिंग फ़्लाइट, बैगेज क्लेम) में धाराप्रवाह संवाद करना।
4. समय की उपलब्धता (Availability), शेड्यूलिंग टकराव (Conflicts) और देरी की जानकारी स्पष्ट रूप से देना।
5. समय और समय-सीमा (Deadlines) से जुड़े वाक्यों में होने वाली अनुवाद की गलतियों को समाप्त करना।

### 2. Pedagogical Instruction (Teach)
#### Hindi Explanation:
दिन 35 में आपका स्वागत है! आज हम समय प्रबंधन, मीटिंग्स और अपॉइंटमेंट तय करने तथा यात्रा (Travel) से जुड़े सभी व्यावहारिक संवाद सीखेंगे।

1. अपॉइंटमेंट तय करना और बदलना:
   - 'I'd like to schedule an appointment with Dr. Rao for Tuesday.'
   - 'Could we postpone / push back our meeting to 3:00 PM?' (मीटिंग को आगे बढ़ाना/टालना)
   - 'Could we bring forward the call to 10:00 AM?' (मीटिंग को पहले करना)

2. 'By' बनाम 'Until' का अंतर (अति महत्वपूर्ण):
   - 'By 5:00 PM': 5:00 बजे से पहले कभी भी (अंतिम समय सीमा - Deadline): 'Submit the report by 5 PM.'
   - 'Until 5:00 PM': 5:00 बजे तक लगातार जारी रहना (Duration): 'I will be in the meeting until 5 PM.'

3. Prepositions of Time:
   - At: निश्चित समय (at 4:30 PM, at noon, at midnight).
   - On: दिन और तारीख (on Monday, on October 15th).
   - In: महीने, साल और अवधि (in May, in 2026, in 10 minutes).

4. यात्रा संवाद (Travel & Transit):
   - 'What is the boarding time for flight AI-102?'
   - 'Which platform does the Shatabdi Express depart from?'
   - 'My flight has a two-hour layover in Dubai.'

#### English Explanation:
Welcome to Day 35! This lesson provides the linguistic toolkit for precision scheduling, calendar coordination, time management, and transit navigation.

Core Pillars:
1. Appointment Lifecycle:
   - Initiating: 'I would like to book a slot for...'
   - Modifying: 'Could we reschedule / push back / move up...?'
   - Confirming: 'Looking forward to our call at 2:00 PM.'
2. The 'By' vs. 'Until' Epistemology:
   - 'By [Time]' denotes a deadline (at or before that instant).
   - 'Until [Time]' denotes continuous state or action up to that point.
3. Temporal Preposition Precision (The At-On-In Pyramid):
   - 'At' for clock times and specific festival periods ('at 3 PM', 'at dawn').
   - 'On' for calendar days and dates ('on Friday', 'on July 4th').
   - 'In' for periods, seasons, months, and future countdowns ('in July', 'in two hours').
4. Travel & Logistics Lexicon:
   - Boarding pass, layover, gate change, departure delay, baggage claim, round-trip ticket.

#### Core Grammar & Pragmatic Rules:
- **Rule 1: The Temporal Preposition Hierarchy** [Prepositional Precision]
  'At' (punctual time), 'On' (calendar day/date), 'In' (enclosing temporal period / duration). Never use '*in Monday*' or '*on 5 PM*'.
- **Rule 2: Deadline 'By' vs. Continuity 'Until'** [Temporal Aspect]
  'By' marks the completion deadline point: 'Finish this by Friday'. 'Until' marks continuous activity up to the boundary: 'I am working until Friday'.
- **Rule 3: Rescheduling Phrasal Verbs** [Business Collocations]
  'Push back / Postpone' = move to a later time. 'Bring forward / Move up' = move to an earlier time.
- **Rule 4: Transit Departure Syntax** [Timetable Aspect]
  Scheduled public transit uses Present Simple with fixed times: 'The train departs at 06:45 from platform 3.'

#### Formula Matrix:
| Subject Group | Formula Structure | English Example | Hindi Meaning |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **Booking Slot** | `I'd like to schedule / arrange a meeting for [Day/Date] at [Time].` | I'd like to schedule a consultation for Thursday at 11:00 AM. | मैं गुरुवार को सुबह 11:00 बजे के लिए परामर्श का समय तय करना चाहूँगा। |
| **Rescheduling** | `Would it be possible to reschedule our call to [New Time]?` | Would it be possible to reschedule our call to 4:00 PM? | क्या हमारे कॉल का समय बदलकर शाम 4:00 बजे करना संभव होगा? |
| **Deadline Specification** | `Subject + must / have to + finish + by + [Time/Date]` | You have to submit the tax documents by Friday noon. | आपको शुक्रवार दोपहर तक टैक्स दस्तावेज़ जमा करने होंगे। |
| **Transit Inquiry** | `What time does [Flight/Train] depart / board from [Gate/Platform]?` | What time does the Rajdhani Express depart from platform 1? | राजधानी एक्सप्रेस प्लेटफ़ॉर्म 1 से किस समय रवाना होती है? |

#### L1 Hindi Speaker Transfer Trap:
- **Trap Name:** The 'By' vs 'Till/Until' Confusion & Preposition Swaps
- **Common Hindi-Interference Pattern:**
```
1. Saying '*Submit the file till 5 PM*' instead of '*by 5 PM*'.
2. Saying '*I will come in Monday*' instead of '*on Monday*'.
3. Saying '*Train will leave on 4 PM*' instead of '*at 4 PM*'.
```
- **Why This Fails in English:** 'Till/Until' means you do the action continuously until 5 PM. A deadline submission is a single action finished before 5 PM ('by 5 PM'). Specific clock times require 'at'.
- **Natural Authentic English Remediation:** 1. 'Submit the file by 5 PM' | 2. 'I will come on Monday' | 3. 'The train leaves at 4 PM'.

### 3. Core Lexicon & Conversational Formulas (Learn)
#### High-Yield Vocabulary:
| Word | Part of Speech | IPA | Hindi Meaning | Example Sentence |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **Appointment** | *Noun* | `/əˈpɔɪnt.mənt/` | नियत मुलाकात / अपॉइंटमेंट | I have a dental appointment tomorrow at nine. *(कल सुबह नौ बजे मेरा डेंटिस्ट से अपॉइंटमेंट है।)* |
| **Reschedule** | *Verb* | `/ˌriːˈskedʒ.uːl/` | समय बदलना / नया समय निर्धारित करना | Could we reschedule our meeting for next week? *(क्या हम अपनी बैठक का समय अगले सप्ताह के लिए बदल सकते हैं?)* |
| **Postpone** | *Verb* | `/pəʊstˈpəʊn/` | टालना / स्थगित करना | The match was postponed due to heavy rain. *(भारी बारिश के कारण मैच स्थगित कर दिया गया था।)* |
| **Deadline** | *Noun* | `/ˈded.laɪn/` | अंतिम समय सीमा | The project deadline is strictly this Friday. *(परियोजना की अंतिम समय सीमा पूरी तरह से इस शुक्रवार है।)* |
| **Layover** | *Noun* | `/ˈleɪˌəʊ.vər/` | यात्रा के दौरान ठहराव (कनेक्टिंग फ़्लाइट का इंतज़ार) | We have a three-hour layover in Singapore. *(सिंगापुर में हमारा तीन घंटे का लेओवर (ठहराव) है।)* |
| **Depart** | *Verb* | `/dɪˈpɑːt/` | रवाना होना / प्रस्थान करना | The express train will depart from platform four. *(एक्सप्रेस ट्रेन प्लेटफॉर्म नंबर चार से रवाना होगी।)* |
| **Boarding** | *Noun* | `/ˈbɔː.dɪŋ/` | विमान या ट्रेन में चढ़ने की प्रक्रिया | Boarding will commence twenty minutes before departure. *(प्रस्थान से बीस मिनट पहले बोर्डिंग शुरू होगी।)* |
| **Punctual** | *Adjective* | `/ˈpʌŋk.tʃu.əl/` | समय का पाबंद / समयनिष्ठ | She is always punctual for our morning standups. *(वह हमारी सुबह की बैठकों के लिए हमेशा समय की पाबंद रहती हैं।)* |
| **Baggage** | *Noun* | `/ˈbæɡ.ɪdʒ/` | सामान / लगेज (Uncountable) | Please collect your checked baggage from carousel three. *(कृपया कन्वेयर बेल्ट तीन से अपना चेक-इन सामान प्राप्त करें।)* |
| **Available** | *Adjective* | `/əˈveɪ.lə.bəl/` | उपलब्ध / खाली | Are you available for a brief sync this afternoon? *(क्या आप आज दोपहर संक्षिप्त बातचीत के लिए उपलब्ध हैं?)* |

#### Essential Pragmatic Phrases:
- **"Could we push back the meeting by 30 minutes?"** [Business Rescheduling]
  *अर्थ:* क्या हम मीटिंग को 30 मिनट आगे खिसका सकते हैं?
- **"I have a tight schedule today."** [Time Management]
  *अर्थ:* आज मेरा शेड्यूल बहुत व्यस्त है।
- **"Let's pencil in Tuesday at 3:00 PM."** [Tentative Scheduling]
  *अर्थ:* चलो मंगलवार दोपहर 3:00 बजे का समय अनंतिम रूप से तय करते हैं।
- **"What is your earliest availability?"** [Inquiry]
  *अर्थ:* आप सबसे पहले कब उपलब्ध हैं?
- **"The flight has been delayed by two hours."** [Transit Update]
  *अर्थ:* उड़ान दो घंटे के लिए विलंबित हो गई है।
- **"Looking forward to seeing you then!"** [Confirmation]
  *अर्थ:* उस समय आपसे मिलने की प्रतीक्षा रहेगी!

### 4. Bilingual Exemplar Models
1. **EN:** I have scheduled an appointment with the dermatologist for Wednesday at 10:30 AM.
   **HI:** मैंने बुधवार को सुबह 10:30 बजे त्वचा विशेषज्ञ के साथ अपॉइंटमेंट तय किया है।
2. **EN:** Due to an unforeseen conflict, I need to reschedule our interview.
   **HI:** एक अप्रत्याशित टकराव के कारण, मुझे हमारे साक्षात्कार का समय बदलना होगा।
3. **EN:** Please ensure all project deliverables are submitted by Friday at 5:00 PM.
   **HI:** कृपया सुनिश्चित करें कि सभी प्रोजेक्ट कार्य शुक्रवार शाम 5:00 बजे तक जमा हो जाएँ।
4. **EN:** I will be out of the office until next Monday.
   **HI:** मैं अगले सोमवार तक कार्यालय से बाहर रहूँगा।
5. **EN:** Our flight to London departs from Gate 24B in forty minutes.
   **HI:** लंदन के लिए हमारी उड़ान चालीस मिनट में गेट 24B से रवाना होगी।
6. **EN:** Does 2:00 PM work for you, or would 4:00 PM be better?
   **HI:** क्या दोपहर 2:00 बजे का समय आपके लिए ठीक रहेगा, या शाम 4:00 बजे बेहतर होगा?
7. **EN:** Passengers for flight 6E-204 are requested to proceed to the boarding gate.
   **HI:** उड़ान 6E-204 के यात्रियों से अनुरोध है कि वे बोर्डिंग गेट पर जाएँ।
8. **EN:** The train arrived on time, exactly at 07:15 AM.
   **HI:** ट्रेन ठीक समय पर, सुबह 07:15 बजे पहुँची।
9. **EN:** We have a two-hour layover at Doha International Airport.
   **HI:** दोहा अंतर्राष्ट्रीय हवाई अड्डे पर हमारा दो घंटे का लेओवर है।
10. **EN:** Can we bring forward our weekly sync to tomorrow morning?
   **HI:** क्या हम अपनी साप्ताहिक बैठक कल सुबह पहले कर सकते हैं?
11. **EN:** I'm running five minutes late due to heavy traffic on the expressway.
   **HI:** एक्सप्रेसवे पर भारी ट्रैफ़िक के कारण मुझे पाँच मिनट की देरी हो रही है।
12. **EN:** Please confirm your hotel reservation before checking in.
   **HI:** चेक-इन करने से पहले कृपया अपने होटल आरक्षण की पुष्टि करें।

### 5. Pronunciation & Phonological Calibration
- **Target Sounds & Suprasegmentals:** Stress patterns in scheduling nouns: 'Ap-POINT-ment' /əˈpɔɪnt.mənt/, 'RES-che-dule' /ˌriːˈskedʒ.uːl/; Flapped /t/ in 'What time does...' /wʌt taɪm dʌz/
- **Phonetic Trap:** Do not pronounce 'schedule' as /sked-yoo-lee/. In British English it is /ˈʃedʒ.uːl/, in American English /ˈskedʒ.uːl/. Avoid pluralizing 'baggages' (it is always uncountable 'baggage').
**Audio Drills:**
- `[day35_audio_001]` (TEACHER): "Could we reschedule our meeting for Friday morning?"
- `[day35_audio_002]` (TEACHER): "Please submit your documents by 5:00 PM."
- `[day35_audio_003]` (TEACHER): "The flight departs from Gate 14 at 09:30."
- `[day35_audio_004]` (TEACHER): "I will be working on this until tomorrow."

### 6. Interactive Practice Drills (35 Items)
**Exercise 1** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: TIME_PREPOSITIONS)
- **Prompt:** Which preposition correctly completes: 'The conference begins ______ Monday morning.'?
- **Options:** `on` | `at` | `in` | `by`
- **Correct Answer:** `on`
- **Explanation (EN):** Days of the week always take preposition 'on' ('on Monday morning').
- **Explanation (HI):** सप्ताह के दिनों के साथ 'on' का प्रयोग होता है।

**Exercise 2** [FILL_IN_BLANK] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: TIME_PREPOSITIONS)
- **Prompt:** Complete the sentence with (at / in): 'Our flight lands ______ 4:30 PM.'
- **Correct Answer:** `at`
- **Explanation (EN):** Exact clock times always require 'at' ('at 4:30 PM').
- **Explanation (HI):** सटीक समय के लिए 'at' आता है।

**Exercise 3** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: DEADLINES)
- **Prompt:** Choose the correct preposition to state a hard deadline: 'Please email the invoice ______ 5:00 PM today.'
- **Options:** `by` | `until` | `for` | `on`
- **Correct Answer:** `by`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'By [Time]' expresses a completion deadline (at or before 5:00 PM).
- **Explanation (HI):** अंतिम समय सीमा (Deadline) दर्शाने के लिए 'by' का प्रयोग होता है।

**Exercise 4** [ERROR_CORRECTION] (Difficulty: medium | Tag: TIME_PREPOSITIONS)
- **Prompt:** Correct the scheduling error: 'I am busy in Tuesday, so let us meet on 3 PM.'
- **Correct Answer:** `I am busy on Tuesday, so let us meet at 3 PM.`
- **Explanation (EN):** Use 'on Tuesday' (day) and 'at 3 PM' (clock time).
- **Explanation (HI):** 'on Tuesday' और 'at 3 PM' सही रूप हैं।

**Exercise 5** [WORD_REORDER] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: RESCHEDULING)
- **Prompt:** Order: [reschedule / could / our meeting / we / for Thursday / ?]
- **Options:** `Could we reschedule our meeting for Thursday?` | `Could reschedule we our meeting for Thursday?` | `Our meeting could we reschedule for Thursday?` | `For Thursday could we reschedule our meeting?`
- **Correct Answer:** `Could we reschedule our meeting for Thursday?`
- **Explanation (EN):** Could we reschedule + our meeting + for Thursday?
- **Explanation (HI):** सही क्रम: Could we reschedule our meeting for Thursday?

**Exercise 6** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: BUSINESS_IDIOMS)
- **Prompt:** What does 'push back a meeting' mean in business English?
- **Options:** `Postpone it to a later time or date.` | `Cancel it permanently.` | `Move it to an earlier time.` | `Change the meeting room.`
- **Correct Answer:** `Postpone it to a later time or date.`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Push back' means postpone or delay to a later time.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'Push back' का अर्थ है मीटिंग को आगे के समय पर टालना।

**Exercise 7** [FILL_IN_BLANK] (Difficulty: medium | Tag: TEMPORAL_ASPECT)
- **Prompt:** Fill in the blank with (until / by): 'I will remain in the office ______ 6:00 PM, and then I will go home.'
- **Correct Answer:** `until`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Until' expresses a continuous state up to a boundary point.
- **Explanation (HI):** लगातार जारी रहने वाली क्रिया के लिए 'until' (तक) आता है।

**Exercise 8** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: TRANSIT_VOCAB)
- **Prompt:** Which term describes a short transit stop between two flights?
- **Options:** `Layover` | `Checkout` | `Boarding` | `Departure`
- **Correct Answer:** `Layover`
- **Explanation (EN):** A stopover/wait between connecting flights is a 'layover'.
- **Explanation (HI):** कनेक्टिंग फ़्लाइट के बीच के ठहराव को 'Layover' कहते हैं।

**Exercise 9** [HINDI_TO_ENGLISH] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: TRANSLATION_HI_EN)
- **Prompt:** Translate: 'क्या हम कॉल को कल सुबह तक टाल सकते हैं?'
- **Options:** `Could we postpone the call until tomorrow morning?` | `Can we throw the call tomorrow morning?` | `Will we cancel call for morning?` | `Delay the call till tomorrow morning.`
- **Correct Answer:** `Could we postpone the call until tomorrow morning?`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Could we postpone the call until tomorrow morning?' is polite and accurate.
- **Explanation (HI):** सही अनुवाद: 'Could we postpone the call until tomorrow morning?'

**Exercise 10** [ERROR_CORRECTION] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: NOUN_COUNTABILITY)
- **Prompt:** Fix the noun countability error: 'I collected all my baggages from the counter.'
- **Correct Answer:** `I collected all my baggage from the counter.`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Baggage' and 'luggage' are uncountable nouns; they never take plural -s.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'Baggage' अगणनीय संज्ञा (Uncountable) है, इसमें 's' नहीं लगता।

**Exercise 11** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: SCHEDULING)
- **Prompt:** Choose the correct phrase to ask if a particular time suits someone:
- **Options:** `Does 3:00 PM work for you?` | `Is 3:00 PM working your body?` | `Do you do 3:00 PM?` | `Are you work on 3:00 PM?`
- **Correct Answer:** `Does 3:00 PM work for you?`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Does [Time] work for you?' is the standard conversational scheduling question.
- **Explanation (HI):** समय की उपयुक्तता पूछने के लिए 'Does 3:00 PM work for you?' सबसे स्वाभाविक है।

**Exercise 12** [FILL_IN_BLANK] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: TIME_PREPOSITIONS)
- **Prompt:** Complete the sentence with the preposition: 'The workshop will take place ______ (in / on) October.'
- **Correct Answer:** `in`
- **Explanation (EN):** Months alone take 'in' ('in October').
- **Explanation (HI):** केवल महीने के नाम के साथ 'in' आता है।

**Exercise 13** [WORD_REORDER] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: SENTENCE_BUILDING)
- **Prompt:** Order: [departs / platform 2 / the express train / from / at 11:15]
- **Options:** `The express train departs from platform 2 at 11:15.` | `At 11:15 the express train departs platform 2 from.` | `Departs the express train from platform 2 at 11:15.` | `From platform 2 departs at 11:15 the express train.`
- **Correct Answer:** `The express train departs from platform 2 at 11:15.`
- **Explanation (EN):** The express train + departs from platform 2 + at 11:15.
- **Explanation (HI):** सही क्रम: The express train departs from platform 2 at 11:15.

**Exercise 14** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: medium | Tag: BUSINESS_IDIOMS)
- **Prompt:** What does 'bring forward a meeting' mean?
- **Options:** `Move it to an earlier time.` | `Move it to a later time.` | `Invite more people.` | `Cancel the agenda.`
- **Correct Answer:** `Move it to an earlier time.`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Bring forward' means reschedule to an earlier time.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'Bring forward' का अर्थ है मीटिंग को पहले (जल्दी) के समय पर रखना।

**Exercise 15** [ENGLISH_TO_HINDI] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: TRANSLATION_EN_HI)
- **Prompt:** What is the meaning of: 'She is very punctual'?
- **Options:** `वह समय की बहुत पाबंद है।` | `वह हमेशा देर से आती है।` | `वह बहुत सख्त है।` | `वह बहुत व्यस्त है।`
- **Correct Answer:** `वह समय की बहुत पाबंद है।`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Punctual' means always arriving on time (समयनिष्ठ).
- **Explanation (HI):** 'Punctual' का अर्थ 'समय का पाबंद' होता है।

**Exercise 16** [FILL_IN_BLANK] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: TIME_PREPOSITIONS)
- **Prompt:** Complete the airline announcement: 'Boarding will commence ______ (in / on) ten minutes.'
- **Correct Answer:** `in`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'In ten minutes' indicates duration until an event begins.
- **Explanation (HI):** भविष्य की अवधि के लिए 'in ten minutes' आएगा।

**Exercise 17** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: PROFESSIONAL_COMMUNICATION)
- **Prompt:** Which message is best to notify a client that you will be 10 minutes late?
- **Options:** `Apologies for the delay; I am stuck in traffic and will arrive in 10 minutes.` | `I am late. Wait for me.` | `Traffic is bad so meeting cancelled.` | `Why did you schedule meeting now?`
- **Correct Answer:** `Apologies for the delay; I am stuck in traffic and will arrive in 10 minutes.`
- **Explanation (EN):** Professional delay update with apology and accurate ETA.
- **Explanation (HI):** पेशेवर शिष्टाचार: क्षमायाचना, कारण और पहुँचने का अनुमानित समय (ETA)।

**Exercise 18** [ERROR_CORRECTION] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: TIME_PREPOSITIONS)
- **Prompt:** Correct the sentence: 'The meeting is on 4:00 o'clock.'
- **Correct Answer:** `The meeting is at 4:00 o'clock.`
- **Explanation (EN):** Use preposition 'at' for clock hours.
- **Explanation (HI):** घड़ी के समय के लिए 'at' आता है ('at 4:00 o'clock')।

**Exercise 19** [DIALOGUE_COMPLETION] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: APPOINTMENTS)
- **Prompt:** Complete the dialogue: Doctor's Receptionist: 'When would you like to come in?' Patient: 'Do you have anything available ______ Thursday afternoon?'
- **Correct Answer:** `on`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'On Thursday afternoon' or 'for Thursday afternoon'.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'On' या 'for' आएगा।

**Exercise 20** [WORD_REORDER] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: SENTENCE_BUILDING)
- **Prompt:** Order: [forward / looking / to / I / am / seeing / then / you]
- **Options:** `I am looking forward to seeing you then.` | `Looking forward I am to seeing you then.` | `To seeing you then I am looking forward.` | `I am to seeing you then looking forward.`
- **Correct Answer:** `I am looking forward to seeing you then.`
- **Explanation (EN):** I am looking forward to + seeing + you + then.
- **Explanation (HI):** सही क्रम: I am looking forward to seeing you then.

**Exercise 21** [FILL_IN_BLANK] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: DEADLINES)
- **Prompt:** Fill in the blank: 'The project must be finished ______ (by / until) the end of this quarter.'
- **Correct Answer:** `by`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'By the end of...' denotes completion before that target deadline.
- **Explanation (HI):** अंतिम समय सीमा के लिए 'by' आएगा।

**Exercise 22** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: TRANSIT_VOCAB)
- **Prompt:** What is the document required to board an airplane after checking in?
- **Options:** `Boarding pass` | `Passport photo` | `Debit card` | `Hotel voucher`
- **Correct Answer:** `Boarding pass`
- **Explanation (EN):** A 'boarding pass' grants entry onto the aircraft.
- **Explanation (HI):** विमान में चढ़ने के लिए 'Boarding pass' अनिवार्य होता है।

**Exercise 23** [ERROR_CORRECTION] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: TIME_PREPOSITIONS)
- **Prompt:** Fix the preposition error: 'We reached Delhi on night.'
- **Correct Answer:** `We reached Delhi at night.`
- **Explanation (EN):** The standard phrase is 'at night' (or 'in the night').
- **Explanation (HI):** रात के समय के लिए 'at night' आता है।

**Exercise 24** [HINDI_TO_ENGLISH] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: TRANSLATION_HI_EN)
- **Prompt:** Translate: 'मेरी ट्रेन प्लेटफ़ॉर्म 3 से छूटती है।'
- **Options:** `My train departs from platform 3.` | `My train is go from platform 3.` | `Train of mine leaving platform 3.` | `Platform 3 has my train departure.`
- **Correct Answer:** `My train departs from platform 3.`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'My train departs from platform 3' is grammatically precise.
- **Explanation (HI):** सही अनुवाद: 'My train departs from platform 3.'

**Exercise 25** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: medium | Tag: IDIOMS)
- **Prompt:** Choose the correct phrase to suggest a flexible, unconfirmed meeting time:
- **Options:** `Let's pencil in Friday at 2:00 PM for now.` | `Friday at 2:00 PM is written in stone.` | `You have to come on Friday 2:00 PM.` | `I command us to meet Friday.`
- **Correct Answer:** `Let's pencil in Friday at 2:00 PM for now.`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Pencil in' is an idiom meaning tentatively schedule an appointment.
- **Explanation (HI):** अनंतिम समय तय करने के लिए 'pencil in' मुहावरे का प्रयोग होता है।

**Exercise 26** [FILL_IN_BLANK] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: TIME_PREPOSITIONS)
- **Prompt:** Complete the sentence: 'She has been working on this report ______ (since / for) 8:00 AM.'
- **Correct Answer:** `since`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Since' is used with specific starting points in the past.
- **Explanation (HI):** निश्चित समय बिंदु (8:00 AM) के लिए 'since' आता है।

**Exercise 27** [WORD_REORDER] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: SENTENCE_BUILDING)
- **Prompt:** Order: [a two-hour / we / layover / in Dubai / have]
- **Options:** `We have a two-hour layover in Dubai.` | `A two-hour layover in Dubai we have.` | `In Dubai have we a two-hour layover.` | `We in Dubai have a two-hour layover.`
- **Correct Answer:** `We have a two-hour layover in Dubai.`
- **Explanation (EN):** We have + a two-hour layover + in Dubai.
- **Explanation (HI):** सही क्रम: We have a two-hour layover in Dubai.

**Exercise 28** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: PROFESSIONAL_COMMUNICATION)
- **Prompt:** Which question politely asks someone when they are next free to speak?
- **Options:** `What is your earliest availability for a quick call?` | `When you are free to talk to me?` | `Why don't you have time for me?` | `Call me when you stop working.`
- **Correct Answer:** `What is your earliest availability for a quick call?`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'What is your earliest availability...?' is polished corporate English.
- **Explanation (HI):** शिष्ट व्यावसायिक पूछताछ: 'What is your earliest availability...?'

**Exercise 29** [ERROR_CORRECTION] (Difficulty: medium | Tag: TEMPORAL_ASPECT)
- **Prompt:** Fix the error: 'I have to wait here until 5 PM to submit it by 5 PM.' (Clarify meaning: 'I have to wait here until the office opens at 10 AM')
- **Correct Answer:** `I have to wait here until the office opens at 10 AM.`
- **Explanation (EN):** Demonstrates continuity with 'until'.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'Until' का सही प्रयोग।

**Exercise 30** [FILL_IN_BLANK] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: TIME_PREPOSITIONS)
- **Prompt:** Fill in the blank with (at / in): 'The flight will land ______ exactly twenty minutes.'
- **Correct Answer:** `in`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'In twenty minutes' = duration countdown from now.
- **Explanation (HI):** भविष्य की अवधि के लिए 'in' आता है।

**Exercise 31** [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: COLLOCATIONS)
- **Prompt:** What is the meaning of 'tight schedule'?
- **Options:** `A very busy and packed calendar with little free time.` | `A loose schedule with lots of breaks.` | `A cancelled itinerary.` | `A schedule written tightly on paper.`
- **Correct Answer:** `A very busy and packed calendar with little free time.`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Tight schedule' means having very little spare time between commitments.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'Tight schedule' का अर्थ है अत्यधिक व्यस्त दिनचर्या।

**Exercise 32** [HINDI_TO_ENGLISH] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: TRANSLATION_HI_EN)
- **Prompt:** Translate: 'कृपया अपनी सीट बेल्ट बाँध लें।' (विमान घोषणा)
- **Options:** `Please fasten your seatbelts.` | `Tie your seatbelts please.` | `Lock your seat belts.` | `Close the seat belt.`
- **Correct Answer:** `Please fasten your seatbelts.`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'Fasten your seatbelts' is standard transit vocabulary.
- **Explanation (HI):** सही अनुवाद: 'Please fasten your seatbelts.'

**Exercise 33** [SPOKEN_RESPONSE] (Difficulty: medium | Tag: SPEAKING)
- **Prompt:** Speak aloud: You need to reschedule a client meeting from Tuesday at 11:00 AM to Thursday at 3:00 PM. Record your explanation, including: 1. A polite apology, 2. The proposed new day and time, 3. An inquiry if that works for them.
- **Correct Answer:** `A fluent 3-part business rescheduling message.`
- **Explanation (EN):** Includes apology, specific alternative time with prepositions ('to Thursday at 3:00 PM'), and confirmation inquiry.
- **Explanation (HI):** शिष्ट क्षमायाचना, नया समय और उपयुक्तता की पुष्टि शामिल करें।

**Exercise 34** [RECALL] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: RECALL)
- **Prompt:** Which preposition is used for specific days and dates ('___ Monday', '___ July 15th')?
- **Correct Answer:** `on`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'On' is used for days and specific dates.
- **Explanation (HI):** दिनों और तारीखों के साथ 'on' का प्रयोग होता है।

**Exercise 35** [RECALL] (Difficulty: easy | Tag: RECALL)
- **Prompt:** What is the difference between 'by 5 PM' and 'until 5 PM' in terms of deadline vs. continuous action?
- **Correct Answer:** `by is a deadline, until is continuous`
- **Explanation (EN):** 'By' denotes a completion point (deadline); 'until' denotes continuous action up to that point.
- **Explanation (HI):** 'By' अंतिम समय सीमा (deadline) है, जबकि 'until' निरंतर अवधि (duration) दर्शाता है।

### 7. Authentic Communicative Application (Use)
- **Real-World Context:** A project manager, Ananya, calls a client, David, to reschedule a sprint review meeting due to an executive board presentation.
**Mastery Dialogue:**
- **Ananya:** "Hello David, thank you for taking my call. I'm calling regarding our sprint review scheduled for tomorrow at 2:00 PM."
  *(हिंदी: "नमस्ते डेविड, मेरा कॉल उठाने के लिए धन्यवाद। मैं कल दोपहर 2:00 बजे होने वाले हमारे स्प्रिंट रिव्यू के संबंध में कॉल कर रही हूँ।")*
- **David:** "Hi Ananya! Is everything on track?"
  *(हिंदी: "नमस्ते अनन्या! क्या सब कुछ सही चल रहा है?")*
- **Ananya:** "The development is completely on schedule, but our leadership team called an emergency board meeting for tomorrow afternoon. Would it be possible to push back our review to Thursday at 11:00 AM?"
  *(हिंदी: "विकास कार्य पूरी तरह समय पर है, लेकिन हमारी लीडरशिप टीम ने कल दोपहर एक आपातकालीन बोर्ड बैठक बुलाई है। क्या हमारे रिव्यू को गुरुवार सुबह 11:00 बजे तक आगे बढ़ाना संभव होगा?")*
- **David:** "Thursday at 11:00 AM works perfectly for me. Please send over the updated calendar invite."
  *(हिंदी: "गुरुवार सुबह 11:00 बजे का समय मेरे लिए बिल्कुल सही है। कृपया अपडेट किया गया कैलेंडर इनवाइट भेज दीजिए।")*
- **Ananya:** "I'll email the invite right away. Thank you so much for your flexibility, David!"
  *(हिंदी: "मैं अभी इनवाइट ईमेल करती हूँ। आपके सहयोग के लिए बहुत-बहुत धन्यवाद, डेविड!")*

**Spoken Production Challenge:**
- **Task (EN):** Simulate a 1-minute phone call booking a medical consultation at a hospital, specifying your preferred date, morning slot, and doctor.
- **Task (HI):** अस्पताल में डॉक्टर से परामर्श का समय तय करने के लिए 1 मिनट के फ़ोन कॉल का अभ्यास करें।

### 8. Rigorous Assessment & Verification
**Assessment Query 1** [Skill: Temporal Semantics]
- **Question:** Contrast the precise grammatical and pragmatic function of 'by' versus 'until' with concrete timeline examples.
- **Model Analytical Benchmark:** 'By [Time]' establishes a deadline before or at which a single completion event must occur ('Submit the report by 5 PM' = submit anytime up to 5 PM). 'Until [Time]' specifies an ongoing continuous state terminating at the time boundary ('I am working until 5 PM' = continuous work from now through 5 PM).

**Assessment Query 2** [Skill: Prepositional Geometry]
- **Question:** Explain the Preposition Hierarchy for time ('At', 'On', 'In') with structural boundaries.
- **Model Analytical Benchmark:** 'At' governs punctual, precise clock times and specific micro-periods ('at 4 PM', 'at midnight'). 'On' governs discrete calendar 24-hour days and dates ('on Tuesday', 'on August 15th'). 'In' governs broad macro-durations, months, years, and forward countdown spans ('in August', 'in 2026', 'in 15 minutes').

**Assessment Query 3** [Skill: Corporate Pragmatics]
- **Question:** Outline a complete, professional five-step protocol for rescheduling a high-stakes business appointment.
- **Model Analytical Benchmark:** 1. Prompt notification, 2. Courteous apology for the imposition, 3. Clear, non-defensive rationale (e.g. 'unforeseen conflict'), 4. Proactive proposal of 2-3 specific alternative slots with exact times, 5. Reconfirmation inquiry and appreciation for flexibility.

### 9. Spaced Repetition Protocol (Review)
- **SRS Module Tag:** `STAGE_4_TIME_APPOINTMENTS_TRAVEL`
- **Spaced Retrieval Cadence:** Days 1, 3, 7, 14, 30
